mwp_dev13_combat_batch_update_slot_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_script_selected_id = 1
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_base_own_maa
            add = scope:mwp_d13_batch_added_capacity
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_base_own_qty_heavy_infantry
            add = 1
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_base_own_chunks_1
            add = 1
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_base_own_damage_heavy_infantry
            add = {
                value = scope:mwp_batch_max_before
                add = scope:mwp_d13_batch_added_capacity
                multiply = var:mwp_damage_$SLOT$
                divide = 1000
                subtract = {
                    value = scope:mwp_batch_max_before
                    multiply = var:mwp_damage_$SLOT$
                    divide = 1000
                }
            }
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_base_own_toughness
            add = {
                value = scope:mwp_batch_max_before
                add = scope:mwp_d13_batch_added_capacity
                multiply = var:mwp_toughness_$SLOT$
                divide = 1000
                subtract = {
                    value = scope:mwp_batch_max_before
                    multiply = var:mwp_toughness_$SLOT$
                    divide = 1000
                }
            }
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_base_own_pursuit
            add = {
                value = scope:mwp_batch_max_before
                add = scope:mwp_d13_batch_added_capacity
                multiply = var:mwp_pursuit_$SLOT$
                divide = 1000
                subtract = {
                    value = scope:mwp_batch_max_before
                    multiply = var:mwp_pursuit_$SLOT$
                    divide = 1000
                }
            }
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_base_own_screen
            add = {
                value = scope:mwp_batch_max_before
                add = scope:mwp_d13_batch_added_capacity
                multiply = var:mwp_screen_$SLOT$
                divide = 1000
                subtract = {
                    value = scope:mwp_batch_max_before
                    multiply = var:mwp_screen_$SLOT$
                    divide = 1000
                }
            }
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_planned_own_maa
            add = scope:mwp_d13_batch_added_capacity
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_planned_own_qty_heavy_infantry
            add = 1
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_planned_own_chunks_1
            add = 1
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_planned_own_damage_heavy_infantry
            add = {
                value = scope:mwp_batch_max_before
                add = scope:mwp_d13_batch_added_capacity
                multiply = var:mwp_damage_$SLOT$
